 577→    \"\"\"\n   578→    Randomly sample trials across all sessions\n   579→    Inputs:\n   580→        data: dict with keys 'neural', 'input', 'output' as described in train_decoder.py\n   581→        nsample: number of trials to sample\n   582→        trial_indices: optional list of length nsessions, trial_indices[session] is a numpy array\n   583→                       with valid trial indices for that session (e.g., test_indices from validate_decoder).\n   584→                       If None, samples from all trials.\n   585→    Returns:\n   586→        sessionplot: numpy array of shape (nsample,), session indices for sampled trials\n   587→        trialplot: numpy array of shape (nsample,), trial indices for sampled trials\n   588→    \"\"\"\n   589→\n   590→    nsessions = len(data['neural'])\n   591→\n   592→    if trial_indices is None:\n   593→        # Sample from all trials\n   594→        ntrials_per_session = [len(data['neural'][i]) for i in range(nsessions)]\n   595→        valid_trials_per_session = [np.arange(n) for n in ntrials_per_session]\n   596→    else:\n   597→        # Sample only from specified trial indices\n   598→        ntrials_per_session = [len(trial_indices[i]) for i in range(nsessions)]\n   599→        valid_trials_per_session = trial_indices\n   600→\n   601→    # Sample sessions with equal probability\n   602→    sessionplot = np.random.choice(nsessions, nsample, replace=nsample > nsessions)\n   603→\n   604→    trialplot = np.full((nsample,), -1, dtype=int)\n   605→    for session in range(nsessions):\n   606→        idx = session == sessionplot\n   607→        ncurr = np.count_nonzero(idx)\n   608→        if ncurr == 0:\n   609→            continue\n   610→        valid_trials = valid_trials_per_session[session]\n   611→        if len(valid_trials) == 0:\n   612→            continue\n   613→        # Sample from valid trials and map back to original trial indices\n   614→        sampled = np.random.choice(len(valid_trials), ncurr, replace=len(valid_trials) < ncurr)\n   615→        trialplot[idx] = valid_trials[sampled]\n   616→\n   617→    return sessionplot, trialplot\n   618→    \n   619→\n   620→def _prepare_session_data(neural: list, input: list, output: list, device, doutput: int = None):\n   621→    \"\"\"\n   622→    Helper function to prepare session data for training/validation.\n   623→\n   624→    Inputs:\n   625→        neural: list of length nsessions, neural[session] is a list of trials\n   626→        input: list of length nsessions, input[session] is a list of trials\n   627→        output: list of length nsessions, output[session] is a list of trials\n   628→        device: torch device to move data to\n   629→        doutput: number of output dimensions (if None, inferred from data)\n   630→\n   631→    Returns:\n   632→        session_data: list of dicts with 'neural', 'input', 'output', 'nneurons' tensors\n   633→        dinput: input dimension\n   634→        doutput: output dimension\n   635→    \"\"\"\n   636→    nsessions = len(neural)\n   637→\n   638→    # Determine dimensions\n   639→    # Find first non-empty session/trial\n   640→    for sess in range(nsessions):\n   641→        if len(neural[sess]) > 0:\n   642→            if input[sess][0].ndim == 1:\n   643→                dinput = len(input[sess][0])\n   644→            else:\n   645→                dinput = input[sess][0].shape[0]\n   646→            if doutput is None:\n   647→                if output[sess][0].ndim == 1:\n   648→                    doutput = len(output[sess][0])\n   649→                else:\n   650→                    doutput = output[sess][0].shape[0]\n   651→            break\n   652→\n   653→    # Process input/output: replicate 1D along time axis\n   654→    input_processed = []\n   655→    output_processed = []\n   656→    for session in range(nsessions):\n   657→        input_session = []\n   658→        output_session = []\n   659→        for trial in range(len(neural[session])):\n   660→            T = neural[session][trial].shape[1]\n   661→            if input[session][trial].ndim == 1:\n   662→                input_trial = np.tile(input[session][trial][:, np.newaxis], (1, T))\n   663→            else:\n   664→                input_trial = input[session][trial]\n   665→            input_session.append(input_trial)\n   666→            if output[session][trial].ndim == 1:\n   667→                output_trial = np.tile(output[session][trial][:, np.newaxis], (1, T))\n   668→            else:\n   669→                output_trial = output[session][trial]\n   670→            output_session.append(output_trial)\n   671→        input_processed.append(input_session)\n   672→        output_processed.append(output_session)\n   673→\n   674→    # Create session data tensors\n   675→    session_data = []\n   676→    for session in range(nsessions):\n   677→        if len(neural[session]) == 0:\n   678→            session_data.append(None)\n   679→            continue\n   680→        neural_concat = np.concatenate([neural[session][trial].T.astype(np.float32) for trial in range(len(neural[session]))], axis=0)\n   681→        input_concat = np.concatenate([input_processed[session][trial].T for trial in range(len(input_processed[session]))], axis=0).astype(np.float32)\n   682→        output_concat = np.concatenate([output_processed[session][trial].T for trial in range(len(output_processed[session]))], axis=0).astype(np.int64)\n   683→        session_data.append({\n   684→            'neural': torch.from_numpy(neural_concat),\n   685→            'input': torch.from_numpy(input_concat),\n   686→            'output': torch.from_numpy(output_concat),\n   687→            'nneurons': neural_concat.shape[1]\n   688→        })\n   689→\n   690→    return session_data, dinput, doutput\n   691→\n   692→\n   693→def train_decoder(neural: list, input: list, output: list, metadata: dict = {}, **kwargs):\n   694→    \"\"\"\n   695→    Trains a common decoder for all sessions to predict output from neural and input data.\n   696→    Jointly learns projection matrices for each session and separate shared decoders for each output dimension.\n   697→\n   698→    Inputs:\n   699→        neural: neural activity. neural is a list of length nsessions, neural[session] is a list\n   700→                of length ntrials[session], and neural[session][trial] is a numpy array of shape (nneurons[session], T[session][trial]),\n   701→                dtype = float32, giving the neural activity for that trial\n   702→        input: variables beyond neural activity the decoder should input, e.g. the stimulis, condition. input is a list of\n   703→                length nsessions, input[session] is a list of length ntrials[session], and input[session][trial] is a numpy array of\n   704→                shape (dinput, T[session][trial]), dtype = float32. with the input variable(s) for each timepoint.\n   705→                dinput is the number of inputs. For discrete inputs, use a one-hot encoding.\n   706→        output: variables we want to decode. output is a list of length nsessions, output[session] is a list of length ntrials[session],\n   707→                and output[session][trial] is a numpy array of shape (doutput, T[session][trial]) of dtype = int with\n   708→                categorical values for each timepoint. Each output[idx, t] is an integer from 0 to ncategories[idx]-1.\n   709→        metadata: optional dict with additional information about the experiment.\n   710→\n   711→        Algorithm hyperparameters can be passed as keyword arguments:\n   712→        npcs: number of projected dimensions to use (default: 10)\n   713→        num_epochs: number of training epochs (default: 200)\n   714→        lr: learning rate (default: 0.001)\n   715→        l1_weight: L1 regularization weight for projection matrices (default: 1e-4)\n   716→        svd_max_samples: maximum number of timepoints to use for SVD initialization (default: 50000).\n   717→                         If a session has more timepoints than this, a random subset will be used.\n   718→        svd_max_neurons: maximum number of neurons to use for SVD initialization (default: 2000).\n   719→                         If a session has more neurons than this, random Gaussian projection will be used.\n   720→                         The final weight is the product of the random projection and SVD matrices.\n   721→        ncategories: optional list of length doutput giving number of categories for each output dimension.\n   722→                     If not provided, will be inferred from data.\n   723→        balanced_loss: if True, use class-weighted cross-entropy loss to compensate for class imbalance.\n   724→                       Weights are inverse of class frequency. Default: False.\n   725→\n   726→    Returns:\n   727→        model: dict with trained decoder parameters. dict with the keys:\n   728→            'projection': list of length nsessions, projection[session] is a torch tensor of shape (npcs, nneurons[session])\n   729→            'decoder': list of length doutput, decoder[out_dim] is a torch linear layer for that output dimension\n   730→            'ncategories': list of length doutput, ncategories[out_dim] is the number of categories for that output\n   731→            'device': torch device used for training\n   732→\n   733→    \"\"\"\n   734→\n   735→    model = {\n   736→        'projection': [],\n   737→        'decoder': [],\n   738→        'ncategories': [],\n   739→        'device': None\n   740→    }\n   741→\n   742→    npcs = kwargs.get('npcs', 10)\n   743→    num_epochs = kwargs.get('num_epochs', 200)\n   744→    lr = kwargs.get('lr', 0.001)\n   745→    l1_weight = kwargs.get('l1_weight', 1e-4)\n   746→    device_str = kwargs.get('device', None)\n   747→    balanced_loss = kwargs.get('balanced_loss', False)\n   748→\n   749→    nsessions = len(neural)\n   750→    if device_str is not None:\n   751→        device = torch.device(device_str)\n   752→    else:\n   753→        device = torch.device('cuda' if torch.cuda.is_available() else 'cpu')\n   754→\n   755→    model['device'] = device\n   756→    print('Using device:', device)\n   757→\n   758→    # Prepare training data using helper function\n   759→    session_data, dinput, doutput = _prepare_session_data(neural, input, output, device)\n   760→\n   761→    # Determine number of categories for each output dimension\n   762→    if 'ncategories' in kwargs:\n   763→        ncategories = kwargs['ncategories']\n   764→    else:\n   765→        ncategories = []\n   766→        for out_dim in range(doutput):\n   767→            max_cat = 0\n   768→            for session in range(nsessions):\n   769→                if session_data[session] is None:\n   770→                    continue\n   771→                max_cat = max(max_cat, int(session_data[session]['output'][:, out_dim].max().item()))\n   772→            ncategories.append(max_cat + 1)\n   773→\n   774→    model['ncategories'] = ncategories\n   775→\n   776→    # Compute class weights (always, for use in chance computation and optionally for balanced loss)\n   777→    class_weights = []\n   778→    class_fractions = []  # Store fractions for chance computation\n   779→    for out_dim in range(doutput):\n   780→        # Count occurrences of each class across all sessions\n   781→        class_counts = np.zeros(ncategories[out_dim])\n   782→        for session in range(nsessions):\n   783→            if session_data[session] is None:\n   784→                continue\n   785→            outputs = session_data[session]['output'][:, out_dim].cpu().numpy()\n   786→            for c in range(ncategories[out_dim]):\n   787→                class_counts[c] += np.sum(outputs == c)\n   788→        # Store class fractions for chance computation\n   789→        total = np.sum(class_counts)\n   790→        fractions = class_counts / total\n   791→        class_fractions.append(fractions)\n   792→        # Compute weights as inverse of frequency (with smoothing to avoid division by zero)\n   793→        weights = total / (ncategories[out_dim] * np.maximum(class_counts, 1))\n   794→        # Normalize weights to sum to 1 (so each output dimension is equally weighted)\n   795→        weights = weights / np.sum(weights)\n   796→        class_weights.append(torch.tensor(weights, dtype=torch.float32, device=device))\n   797→\n   798→    model['class_weights'] = class_weights\n   799→    model['class_fractions'] = class_fractions\n   800→\n   801→    if balanced_loss:\n   802→        print(f\"Using balanced loss with class weights:\")\n   803→        for out_dim in range(doutput):\n   804→            print(f\"  Output {out_dim}: {class_weights[out_dim].cpu().numpy()}\")\n   805→\n   806→    # Initialize projection matrices for each session\n   807→    svd_max_samples = kwargs.get('svd_max_samples', 50000)  # Max timepoints to use for SVD initialization\n   808→    svd_max_neurons = kwargs.get('svd_max_neurons', 2000)   # Max neurons to use for SVD initialization\n   809→    projection_layers = []\n   810→    for session in range(nsessions):\n   811→        nneurons = session_data[session]['nneurons']\n   812→        proj = nn.Linear(nneurons, npcs, bias=False).to(device)\n   813→\n   814→        # Initialize with SVD for stability (use V from SVD of neural data)\n   815→        # For neural data with shape (n_timepoints, n_neurons), SVD gives principal components in V\n   816→        # Use random subsets if data is too large\n   817→        n_timepoints = session_data[session]['neural'].shape[0]\n   818→\n   819→        # Sample timepoints if needed\n   820→        if n_timepoints > svd_max_samples:\n   821→            time_indices = torch.randperm(n_timepoints)[:svd_max_samples]\n   822→            neural_subset = session_data[session]['neural'][time_indices, :]\n   823→            print(f\"Session {session}: Using {svd_max_samples} of {n_timepoints} timepoints for SVD initialization\")\n   824→        else:\n   825→            neural_subset = session_data[session]['neural']\n   826→\n   827→        # Use random projection if too many neurons\n   828→        if nneurons > svd_max_neurons:\n   829→            # Create random Gaussian projection matrix: (n_neurons, svd_max_neurons)\n   830→            P = torch.randn(nneurons, svd_max_neurons)\n   831→            # Project neural data: (n_timepoints, n_neurons) @ (n_neurons, svd_max_neurons) = (n_timepoints, svd_max_neurons)\n   832→            neural_projected = neural_subset @ P\n   833→            print(f\"Session {session}: Using random projection to {svd_max_neurons} of {nneurons} neurons for SVD initialization\")\n   834→\n   835→            # SVD on projected data\n   836→            _, _, V = torch.svd(neural_projected, some=True)\n   837→            # V has shape (svd_max_neurons, min(n_timepoints_subset, svd_max_neurons))\n   838→\n   839→            # Compose: final weight = (P @ V[:, :npcs]).T = V[:, :npcs].T @ P.T\n   840→            n_components = min(npcs, V.shape[1])\n   841→            proj.weight.data[:n_components, :] = (V[:, :n_components].T @ P.T).to(device).clone()\n   842→        else:\n   843→            # Standard SVD without random projection\n   844→            _, _, V = torch.svd(neural_subset, some=True)\n   845→            # V has shape (n_neurons, min(n_timepoints_subset, n_neurons))\n   846→            n_components = min(npcs, V.shape[1])\n   847→            proj.weight.data[:n_components, :] = V[:, :n_components].T.to(device).clone()\n   848→\n   849→        # Initialize remaining components randomly if needed\n   850→        if n_components < npcs:\n   851→            nn.init.orthogonal_(proj.weight.data[n_components:, :])\n   852→\n   853→        projection_layers.append(proj)\n   854→\n   855→    # Initialize separate decoders for each output dimension\n   856→    decoders = []\n   857→    for out_dim in range(doutput):\n   858→        decoder = nn.Linear(npcs + dinput, ncategories[out_dim]).to(device)\n   859→        decoders.append(decoder)\n   860→\n   861→    # Collect all parameters\n   862→    all_params = []\n   863→    for proj in projection_layers:\n   864→        all_params.extend(proj.parameters())\n   865→    for decoder in decoders:\n   866→        all_params.extend(decoder.parameters())\n   867→\n   868→    optimizer = optim.Adam(all_params, lr=lr)\n   869→\n   870→    # Loss function with reduction='none' for manual weighting (matches torch_brain)\n   871→    criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(reduction='none')\n   872→\n   873→    # Training loop\n   874→    # Data stays on CPU; each session is moved to GPU one at a time.\n   875→    # backward() is called per session to free the computation graph before\n   876→    # loading the next session. Gradients accumulate across sessions.\n   877→    for epoch in range(num_epochs):\n   878→        epoch_loss = 0.\n   879→        optimizer.zero_grad()\n   880→\n   881→        for session in range(nsessions):\n   882→            if session_data[session] is None:\n   883→                continue\n   884→            # Move this session's data to device\n   885→            neural_batch = session_data[session]['neural'].to(device)\n   886→            input_batch = session_data[session]['input'].to(device)\n   887→            output_batch = session_data[session]['output'].to(device)\n   888→\n   889→            # Forward pass - shared projection\n   890→            projected = projection_layers[session](neural_batch)\n   891→            combined = torch.cat([projected, input_batch], dim=1)\n   892→\n   893→            # Compute loss for each output dimension\n   894→            session_loss = torch.tensor(0., device=device)\n   895→            for out_dim in range(doutput):\n   896→                logits = decoders[out_dim](combined)\n   897→                target = output_batch[:, out_dim]\n   898→                loss_per_sample = criterion(logits, target)\n   899→\n   900→                if balanced_loss:\n   901→                    # Manual per-sample weighting (matches torch_brain exactly)\n   902→                    sample_weights = class_weights[out_dim][target]\n   903→                    session_loss += (sample_weights * loss_per_sample).sum() / sample_weights.sum()\n   904→                else:\n   905→                    session_loss += loss_per_sample.mean()\n   906→\n   907→            # Add L1 regularization on current session's projection matrix\n   908→            if l1_weight > 0:\n   909→                l1_loss = torch.sum(torch.abs(projection_layers[session].weight))\n   910→                session_loss += l1_weight * l1_loss\n   911→\n   912→            # Backward per session: frees computation graph, accumulates gradients\n   913→            session_loss.backward()\n   914→            epoch_loss += session_loss.item()\n   915→\n   916→        optimizer.step()\n   917→\n   918→        if ((epoch + 1) % 10 == 0) or (epoch < 10):\n   919→            # Loss is sum of per-output-dim means across sessions\n   920→            # Normalize by (nsessions * doutput) to get average per output per session\n   921→            train_loss_normalized = epoch_loss / (nsessions * doutput)\n   922→            print(f'Epoch [{epoch+1}/{num_epochs}], Loss: {train_loss_normalized:.6f}')\n   923→\n   924→    # Store trained parameters\n   925→    for session in range(nsessions):\n   926→        model['projection'].append(projection_layers[session].weight.data.clone())\n   927→    model['decoder'] = decoders\n   928→\n   929→    return model\n   930→\n   931→def predict(neural: list, input: list, model: dict, sessionid: list | None = None, metadata: dict = {}):\n   932→    \"\"\"\n   933→    Uses a trained decoder model to predict output from neural and input data.\n   934→\n   935→    Inputs:\n   936→        neural: neural activity. neural is a list of length nsessions, neural[session] is a list\n   937→                of length ntrials[session], and neural[session][trial] is a numpy array of shape (nneurons[session], T[session][trial]),\n   938→                dtype = float32, giving the neural activity for that trial\n   939→        input: variables beyond neural activity the decoder should input, e.g. the stimulis, condition. input is a list of\n   940→                length nsessions, input[session] is a list of length ntrials[session], and input[session][trial] is a numpy array of\n   941→                shape (dinput, T[session][trial]), dtype = float32. with the input variable(s) for each timepoint.\n   942→                dinput is the number of inputs. For discrete inputs, use a one-hot encoding.\n   943→        model: dict with trained decoder parameters. dict with the keys:\n   944→                'projection': list of length nsessions, projection[session] is a torch tensor\n   945→                'decoder': list of length doutput, decoder[out_dim] is a torch linear layer for that output dimension\n   946→                'ncategories': list of length doutput with number of categories per output dimension\n   947→                'device': torch device\n   948→        sessionid: optional list of length nsessions, giving the IDs of the sessions.\n   949→        metadata: optional dict with additional information about the experiment.\n   950→    Returns:\n   951→        predictions: list of length nsessions, predictions[session] is a list of length ntrials[session],\n   952→                and predictions[session][trial] is a numpy array of shape (doutput, T[session][trial]) of dtype = int\n   953→                with the predicted categorical output variable(s) for each timepoint.\n   954→        pcs: list of length nsessions, pcs[session] is a list of length ntrials[session],\n   955→                and pcs[session][trial] is a numpy array of shape (npcs, T[session][trial]) with the projected neural data.\n   956→        confidences: list of length nsessions, confidences[session] is a list of length ntrials[session],\n   957→                and confidences[session][trial] is a numpy array of shape (doutput, T[session][trial]) with the confidence scores.\n   958→    \"\"\"\n   959→\n   960→    nsessions = len(neural)\n   961→    device = model['device']\n   962→    decoders = model['decoder']\n   963→    doutput = len(decoders)\n   964→\n   965→    # Replicate 1D inputs along time axis to match neural data (make copies to avoid modifying original)\n   966→    input_processed = []\n   967→    for session in range(nsessions):\n   968→        input_session = []\n   969→        for trial in range(len(neural[session])):\n   970→            T = neural[session][trial].shape[1]  # time dimension\n   971→\n   972→            # Handle 1D input - replicate along time\n   973→            if input[session][trial].ndim == 1:\n   974→                input_trial = np.tile(input[session][trial][:, np.newaxis], (1, T))\n   975→            else:\n   976→                input_trial = input[session][trial]\n   977→            input_session.append(input_trial)\n   978→        input_processed.append(input_session)\n   979→\n   980→    # Use processed data instead of original\n   981→    input = input_processed\n   982→\n   983→    confidences = []\n   984→    predictions = []\n   985→    pcs = []\n   986→\n   987→    with torch.no_grad():\n   988→        for sessionidx in range(nsessions):\n   989→            if sessionid is not None:\n   990→                session = sessionid[sessionidx]\n   991→            else:\n   992→                session = sessionidx\n   993→\n   994→            ntrials = len(neural[sessionidx])\n   995→            session_predictions = []\n   996→            session_confidences = []\n   997→            session_pcs = []\n   998→            projection = model['projection'][session]\n   999→\n  1000→            for trial in range(ntrials):\n  1001→                # Get trial data\n  1002→                neural_trial = torch.from_numpy(neural[sessionidx][trial].T.astype(np.float32)).to(device)  # (T, nneurons)\n  1003→                input_trial = torch.from_numpy(input[sessionidx][trial].T.astype(np.float32)).to(device)    # (T, dinput)\n  1004→\n  1005→                # Project neural data\n  1006→                projected = torch.matmul(neural_trial, projection.T)  # (T, npcs)\n  1007→\n  1008→                # Decode each output dimension\n  1009→                combined = torch.cat([projected, input_trial], dim=1)  # (T, npcs + dinput)\n  1010→                pred = np.zeros((neural_trial.shape[0], doutput), dtype=np.int64)  # (T, doutput)\n  1011→                conf = np.zeros((neural_trial.shape[0], doutput), dtype=np.float32)\n  1012→\n  1013→                for out_dim in range(doutput):\n  1014→                    logits = decoders[out_dim](combined)  # (T, ncategories[out_dim])\n  1015→                    pred[:, out_dim] = torch.argmax(logits, dim=1).cpu().numpy()\n  1016→                    prob = torch.softmax(logits, dim=1)\n  1017→                    conf[:, out_dim] = prob.max(dim=1).values.cpu().numpy()\n  1018→\n  1019→                # Store results\n  1020→                session_predictions.append(pred.T)  # (doutput, T)\n  1021→                session_confidences.append(conf.T)  # (doutput, T)\n  1022→                session_pcs.append(projected.cpu().numpy().T)  # (npcs, T)\n  1023→\n  1024→            predictions.append(session_predictions)\n  1025→            pcs.append(session_pcs)\n  1026→            confidences.append(session_confidences)\n  1027→\n  1028→    return predictions, pcs, confidences\n  1029→\n  1030→def f1scores_all_sessions(all_predictions: list, output: list):\n  1031→    \"\"\"\n  1032→    Concatenate all predictions and ground truth to compute F1 scores across all sessions and trials\n  1033→    Inputs:\n  1034→        all_predictions: list of length nsessions, all_predictions[session] is a list of length ntrials[session],\n  1035→                and all_predictions[session][trial] is a numpy array of shape (doutput, T[session][trial]) of dtype = bool\n  1036→                with the predicted output variable(s) for each timepoint.\n  1037→        output: the ground truth output for each trial. output is a list of length nsessions, output[session] is a list of\n  1038→                length ntrials[session], and output[session][trial] is a numpy array of shape (doutput, T[session][trial])\n  1039→                OR (doutput,) (scalar per trial), dtype = float32. with the output variable(s) for each timepoint.\n  1040→    Returns:\n  1041→        f1scores: numpy array of shape (doutput,) with the F1 score for each output dimension\n  1042→    \"\"\"\n  1043→    nsessions = len(all_predictions)\n  1044→    all_pred_concat = []\n  1045→    all_output_concat = []\n  1046→    doutput = output[0][0].shape[0]\n  1047→\n  1048→    for session in range(nsessions):\n  1049→        for trial in range(len(all_predictions[session])):\n  1050→            # Predictions are always (doutput, T)\n  1051→            pred_trial = all_predictions[session][trial].T  # (T, doutput)\n  1052→            all_pred_concat.append(pred_trial)\n  1053→\n  1054→            # Output can be (doutput,) or (doutput, T)\n  1055→            output_trial = output[session][trial]\n  1056→            if output_trial.ndim == 1:\n  1057→                # Scalar per trial: (doutput,) -> expand to (T, doutput) where all T are the same\n  1058→                T = pred_trial.shape[0]\n  1059→                output_trial_expanded = np.tile(output_trial[:, np.newaxis], (1, T)).T  # (T, doutput)\n  1060→                all_output_concat.append(output_trial_expanded)\n  1061→            else:\n  1062→                # Time-varying: (doutput, T) -> (T, doutput)\n  1063→                all_output_concat.append(output_trial.T)\n  1064→\n  1065→    # Concatenate all timepoints from all trials and all sessions\n  1066→    all_pred_concat = np.vstack(all_pred_concat)  # (total_timepoints, doutput)\n  1067→    all_output_concat = np.vstack(all_output_concat)  # (total_timepoints, doutput)\n  1068→\n  1069→    # Compute F1 score for each output dimension (macro average for multiclass)\n  1070→    f1scores = np.zeros(doutput)\n  1071→    for out_dim in range(doutput):\n  1072→        f1scores[out_dim] = f1_score(all_output_concat[:, out_dim], all_pred_concat[:, out_dim], average='macro')\n  1073→\n  1074→    return f1scores\n  1075→\n  1076→def accuracy_all_sessions(all_predictions: list, output: list, balanced: bool = False):\n  1077→    \"\"\"\n  1078→    Concatenate all predictions and ground truth to compute accuracy across all sessions and trials.\n  1079→    For categorical outputs.\n  1080→\n  1081→    Inputs:\n  1082→        all_predictions: list of length nsessions, all_predictions[session] is a list of length ntrials[session],\n  1083→                and all_predictions[session][trial] is a numpy array of shape (doutput, T[session][trial]) of dtype = int\n  1084→                with the predicted categorical output variable(s) for each timepoint.\n  1085→        output: the ground truth output for each trial. output is a list of length nsessions, output[session] is a list of\n  1086→                length ntrials[session], and output[session][trial] is a numpy array of shape (doutput, T[session][trial])\n  1087→                OR (doutput,) (scalar per trial), dtype = int with the categorical output variable(s) for each timepoint.\n  1088→        balanced: if True, compute balanced accuracy (average recall per class) to compensate for class imbalance.\n  1089→                  Default: False (standard accuracy).\n  1090→    Returns:\n  1091→        accuracies: numpy array of shape (doutput,) with the accuracy for each output dimension\n  1092→    \"\"\"\n  1093→    from sklearn.metrics import balanced_accuracy_score\n  1094→\n  1095→    nsessions = len(all_predictions)\n  1096→    all_pred_concat = []\n  1097→    all_output_concat = []\n  1098→    doutput = output[0][0].shape[0]\n  1099→\n  1100→    for session in range(nsessions):\n  1101→        for trial in range(len(all_predictions[session])):\n  1102→            # Predictions are always (doutput, T)\n  1103→            pred_trial = all_predictions[session][trial].T  # (T, doutput)\n  1104→            all_pred_concat.append(pred_trial)\n  1105→\n  1106→            # Output can be (doutput,) or (doutput, T)\n  1107→            output_trial = output[session][trial]\n  1108→            if output_trial.ndim == 1:\n  1109→                # Scalar per trial: (doutput,) -> expand to (T, doutput) where all T are the same\n  1110→                T = pred_trial.shape[0]\n  1111→                output_trial_expanded = np.tile(output_trial[:, np.newaxis], (1, T)).T  # (T, doutput)\n  1112→                all_output_concat.append(output_trial_expanded)\n  1113→            else:\n  1114→                # Time-varying: (doutput, T) -> (T, doutput)\n  1115→                all_output_concat.append(output_trial.T)\n  1116→\n  1117→    # Concatenate all timepoints from all trials and all sessions\n  1118→    all_pred_concat = np.vstack(all_pred_concat)  # (total_timepoints, doutput)\n  1119→    all_output_concat = np.vstack(all_output_concat)  # (total_timepoints, doutput)\n  1120→\n  1121→    # Compute accuracy for each output dimension\n  1122→    accuracies = np.zeros(doutput)\n  1123→    for out_dim in range(doutput):\n  1124→        if balanced:\n  1125→            accuracies[out_dim] = balanced_accuracy_score(all_output_concat[:, out_dim], all_pred_concat[:, out_dim])\n  1126→        else:\n  1127→            accuracies[out_dim] = np.mean(all_pred_concat[:, out_dim] == all_output_concat[:, out_dim])\n  1128→\n  1129→    return accuracies\n  1130→\n  1131→def cross_validate_decoder(neural: list, input: list, output: list, metadata: str | None = None,\n  1132→                           nsets: int = 5,\n  1133→                           eval_fn=None,\n  1134→                           **kwargs):\n  1135→    \"\"\"\n  1136→    Cross-validates the decoder function. Performs nsets-fold cross-validation, training the decoder on nsets-1 sets and testing on\n  1137→    the held-out set. Each cross-validation set contains 1/nsets of the trials from each session.\n  1138→    Finds a random split of trials for each session into nsets sets, then, for each set, trains the decoder on the other nsets-1 sets and\n  1139→    computes the predictions on the held out set.\n  1140→    Then computes evaluation metric (F1 score or accuracy) for each output dimension\n  1141→    Inputs:\n  1142→        neural: neural activity. neural is a list of length nsessions, neural[session] is a list\n  1143→                of length ntrials[session], and neural[session][trial] is a numpy array of shape (nneurons[session], T[session][trial]),\n  1144→                dtype = float32, giving the neural activity for that trial\n  1145→        input: variables beyond neural activity the decoder should input, e.g. the stimulis, condition. input is a list of\n  1146→                length nsessions, input[session] is a list of length ntrials[session], and input[session][trial] is a numpy array of\n  1147→                shape (dinput, T[session][trial]), dtype = float32. with the input variable(s) for each timepoint.\n  1148→                dinput is the number of inputs. For discrete inputs, use a one-hot encoding.\n  1149→        output: the ground truth output for each trial. output is a list of length nsessions, output[session] is a list of\n  1150→                length ntrials[session], and output[session][trial] is a numpy array of shape (doutput, T[session][trial]),\n  1151→                dtype = float32 or int. with the output variable(s) for each timepoint.\n  1152→        metadata: optional dict with additional information about the experiment.\n  1153→        train_decoder: function to train the decoder. Should have the signature\n  1154→                model = train_decoder(neural_train, input_train, output_train, metadata, **kwargs)\n  1155→                Default: train_decoder_linear\n  1156→        predict: function to predict using the trained decoder. Should have the signature\n  1157→                predictions = predict(neural_test, input_test, model, sessionid, metadata)\n  1158→                Default: predict_linear\n  1159→        nsets: number of cross-validation sets (default: 5)\n  1160→        eval_fn: evaluation function to compute performance metric. Should have the signature\n  1161→                scores = eval_fn(predictions, output)\n  1162→                Default: accuracy_all_sessions (for binary outputs), can use accuracy_all_sessions for categorical\n  1163→    kwargs are passed to train_decoder\n  1164→    Returns:\n  1165→        scores: numpy array of shape (doutput,) with the evaluation metric for each output dimension\n  1166→        predictions: list of length nsessions, predictions[session] is a list of length ntrials[session],\n  1167→                and predictions[session][trial] is a numpy array of shape (doutput, T[session][trial])\n  1168→                with the predicted output variable(s) for each timepoint.\n  1169→        pcs: list of length nsessions, pcs[session] is a list of length ntrials[session],\n  1170→                and pcs[session][trial] is a numpy array of shape (npcs, T[session][trial]) with the projected neural data.\n  1171→        confidences: list of length nsessions, confidences[session] is a list of length ntrials[session],\n  1172→                and confidences[session][trial] is a numpy array of shape (doutput, T[session][trial]) with the confidence scores.\n  1173→        trial_splits: list of length nsessions, trial_splits[session] is a list of length nsets,\n  1174→                trial_splits[session][set] is a numpy array with the trial indices for that set for that session.\n  1175→    \"\"\"\n  1176→\n  1177→    if eval_fn is None:\n  1178→        eval_fn = accuracy_all_sessions\n  1179→\n  1180→    nsessions = len(neural)\n  1181→\n  1182→    # Create random splits of trials for each session\n  1183→    trial_splits = []\n  1184→    for session in range(nsessions):\n  1185→        ntrials = len(neural[session])\n  1186→        trial_indices = np.random.permutation(ntrials)\n  1187→\n  1188→        # Split trial indices into nsets\n  1189→        split_indices = np.array_split(trial_indices, nsets)\n  1190→        trial_splits.append(split_indices)\n  1191→\n  1192→    # Initialize storage for all predictions (will be filled in during cross-validation)\n  1193→    all_predictions = [[None for _ in range(len(neural[session]))] for session in range(nsessions)]\n  1194→    all_pcs = [[None for _ in range(len(neural[session]))] for session in range(nsessions)]\n  1195→    all_confidences = [[None for _ in range(len(neural[session]))] for session in range(nsessions)]\n  1196→\n  1197→    # Perform cross-validation\n  1198→    for test_set in tqdm.trange(nsets):\n  1199→        # Create training and test data for this fold\n  1200→        train_neural = [[] for _ in range(nsessions)]\n  1201→        train_input = [[] for _ in range(nsessions)]\n  1202→        train_output = [[] for _ in range(nsessions)]\n  1203→\n  1204→        test_neural = [[] for _ in range(nsessions)]\n  1205→        test_input = [[] for _ in range(nsessions)]\n  1206→        test_output = [[] for _ in range(nsessions)]\n  1207→\n  1208→        test_trial_indices = [[] for _ in range(nsessions)]\n  1209→\n  1210→        for session in range(nsessions):\n  1211→            for set_idx in range(nsets):\n  1212→                trials_in_set = trial_splits[session][set_idx]\n  1213→\n  1214→                if set_idx == test_set:\n  1215→                    # These trials go to test set\n  1216→                    for trial_idx in trials_in_set:\n  1217→                        test_neural[session].append(neural[session][trial_idx])\n  1218→                        test_input[session].append(input[session][trial_idx])\n  1219→                        test_output[session].append(output[session][trial_idx])\n  1220→                        test_trial_indices[session].append(trial_idx)\n  1221→                else:\n  1222→                    # These trials go to training set\n  1223→                    for trial_idx in trials_in_set:\n  1224→                        train_neural[session].append(neural[session][trial_idx])\n  1225→                        train_input[session].append(input[session][trial_idx])\n  1226→                        train_output[session].append(output[session][trial_idx])\n  1227→\n  1228→        # 1430→        x_selected.append(x_session_selected)\n  1431→    return x_selected\n  1432→\n  1433→def _train_decoder_pca_logistic(neural: list, input: list, output: list, metadata: dict = {}, **kwargs):\n  1434→    \"\"\"\n  1435→    Trains a common decoder for all sessions to predict output from neural and input data.\n  1436→    Concatenates data across trials for each session, then projects the neural data for each session onto its first npcs principal components.\n  1437→    Then, concatenates data across all sessions and trains a common decoder across all sessions using logistic regression to predict output from the\n  1438→    projected neural data and input data.\n  1439→\n  1440→    Inputs:\n  1441→        neural: neural activity. neural is a list of length nsessions, neural[session] is a list\n  1442→                of length ntrials[session], and neural[session][trial] is a numpy array of shape (nneurons[session], T[session][trial]),\n  1443→                dtype = float32, giving the neural activity for that trial\n  1444→        input: variables beyond neural activity the decoder should input, e.g. the stimulis, condition. input is a list of\n  1445→                length nsessions, input[session] is a list of length ntrials[session], and input[session][trial] is a numpy array of\n  1446→                shape (dinput, T[session][trial]), dtype = float32. with the input variable(s) for each timepoint.\n  1447→                dinput is the number of inputs. For discrete inputs, use a one-hot encoding.\n  1448→        output: variables we want to decode. output is a list of length nsessions, output[session] is a list of length ntrials[session],\n  1449→                and output[session][trial] is a numpy array of shape (doutput, T[session][trial]) of dtype = bool with the\n  1450→                binary output variable(s) for each timepoint. Use a one-hot encoding for multi-class outputs.\n  1451→        metadata: optional dict with additional information about the experiment.\n  1452→\n  1453→        Algorithm hyperparameters can be passed as keyword arguments:\n  1454→        npcs: number of principal components to use (default: 10)\n  1455→\n  1456→    Returns:\n  1457→        model: dict with trained decoder parameters. dict with the keys:\n  1458→            'pca': list of length nsessions, pca[session] is a PCA object fitted to the neural data of that session\n  1459→            'logisticregression': list of length doutput, logisticregression[out_dim] is a sklearn LogisticRegression model\n  1460→\n  1461→    \"\"\"\n  1462→\n  1463→    model = {\n  1464→        'pca': [],\n  1465→        'logisticregression': []\n  1466→    }\n  1467→\n  1468→    npcs = kwargs.get('npcs', 10)\n  1469→    nsessions = len(neural)\n  1470→\n  1471→    # Replicate 1D inputs/outputs along time axis to match neural data (make copies to avoid modifying original)\n  1472→    input_processed = []\n  1473→    output_processed = []\n  1474→    for session in range(nsessions):\n  1475→        input_session = []\n  1476→        output_session = []\n  1477→        for trial in range(len(neural[session])):\n  1478→            T = neural[session][trial].shape[1]  # time dimension\n  1479→\n  1480→            # Handle 1D input - replicate along time\n  1481→            if input[session][trial].ndim == 1:\n  1482→                input_trial = np.tile(input[session][trial][:, np.newaxis], (1, T))\n  1483→            else:\n  1484→                input_trial = input[session][trial]\n  1485→            input_session.append(input_trial)\n  1486→\n  1487→            # Handle 1D output - replicate along time\n  1488→            if output[session][trial].ndim == 1:\n  1489→                output_trial = np.tile(output[session][trial][:, np.newaxis], (1, T))\n  1490→            else:\n  1491→                output_trial = output[session][trial]\n  1492→            output_session.append(output_trial)\n  1493→\n  1494→        input_processed.append(input_session)\n  1495→        output_processed.append(output_session)\n  1496→\n  1497→    # Use processed data instead of original\n  1498→    input = input_processed\n  1499→    output = output_processed\n  1500→\n  1501→    # Store projected neural data and inputs/outputs for all sessions\n  1502→    all_projected_neural = []\n  1503→    all_input = []\n  1504→    all_output = []\n  1505→\n  1506→    # Process each session separately\n  1507→    for session in range(nsessions):\n  1508→        # Concatenate across trials for this session\n  1509→        # neural[session][trial] is (nneurons, T), we want (T_total, nneurons)\n  1510→        neural_concat = np.concatenate([neural[session][trial].T for trial in range(len(neural[session]))], axis=0).astype(np.float32)\n  1511→        input_concat = np.concatenate([input[session][trial].T for trial in range(len(input[session]))], axis=0).astype(np.float32)\n  1512→        output_concat = np.concatenate([output[session][trial].T for trial in range(len(output[session]))], axis=0).astype(bool)\n  1513→\n  1514→        # Project neural data onto first npcs principal components\n  1515→        pca = PCA(n_components=min(npcs, neural_concat.shape[1]))\n  1516→        model['pca'].append(pca)\n  1517→        \n  1518→        neural_projected = pca.fit_transform(neural_concat)\n  1519→\n  1520→        #
